# Community-layer report (S38): tables

Full study batch: studies G, G10, H, I and J (the community layer), compared with studies A, B and B10 (the same rows without the layer, and the no-programme rows), seeds 1-500, engine `ce8d030`, the frozen engine on every row. Generated by `python3 dev/tools/s38_layer.py <compassionism-sim-results checkout> dev/runs/s38-layer` from BetterToBest/compassionism-sim-results `prog-{g,g10,h,i,j,a,b,b10}-ce8d030`. Every interval is a 95% paired-bootstrap interval over seeds (10,000 replicates); comparisons between population sizes are independent runs. For the three shares a reduction is a positive number of points, so a positive layer effect means the layer helps; for cost and inflation positive means more cost or more inflation. Flags: † interval excludes zero; M also material (1 point; 5% of the release row's cost, or the full design's cost for contrasts; 1 point of the yearly inflation rate); H survives Holm in its preregistered family. Every estimate is also in `estimates.csv`.

## 0. Checks

- PASS  rows per environment: G 72 (36 x 2 horizons), G10 32, H 36 (18 x 2), I 24 (12 x 2), J 8 (4 x 2 sizes)
- PASS  G10 and J: the release row with the layer at 10,000 adults, 20 years, is identical in both studies, every key and seed (6 rows): 0 differ
- PASS  B10 against A: the release and none rows at 10,000 adults, 20 years, CPS, are identical, every key and seed (12 rows): 0 differ
- PASS  G, H, I, J: in every 40-year row the year-20 measures equal the 20-year row, seed by seed: 0 of 840 differ
- PASS  G, G10, H: the 84 rows without the CCO are identical under the two designs (but the C2.2 reporting keys): 0 differ
- PASS  every row with the layer has adults / k communities on every seed (5 at 500 adults, 20 at 2,000, 100 at 10,000; k 50 and 250 in H): 0 rows differ
- PASS  Shapley values add up to v(all five), as linear maps
- PASS  per seed, in every setting and outcome, the Shapley values with the layer add up to v(all five) (largest relative gap 7.2e-16)
- PASS  the factorial's full cell with the layer is the release row with the layer of Q7, seed by seed (18 settings): 0 differ

## 3. Synergy with the layer (Q8)

The study programme's Shapley values and interaction contrasts on the factorial with the layer on: the 16 cells with PTF from G (500 adults) or G10 (10,000 adults), the 15 cells without PTF and the none row from B or B10 (identical with the layer on, addendum check 2). CPS yardstick, raise off. Δ is the estimate with the layer minus the estimate with the layer off (B or B10), paired. The pairs the layer is built to test are PTF × SZH and CIP × SZH. Headline: G10 at 10,000 adults (20 years).

## 4. Design parameters (Q9, study H)

Each swept row minus the main-value row (k 100, f 0.5, local imitation, zones without CIP), paired, 500 adults, CPS, raise off. For the four `zc` rows the main-value row is the same cell with the layer at its main values (G). Holm over the nine rows of each outcome. "Decision differs" lists any outcome whose decision under the addendum's rule 1 (the layer's effect: row against the same row without the layer) or the programme's rule 1 (row against none) differs from the main-value row.

## 5. PTF starting size (Q10, study I)

The layer's effect (with − without, paired) at a PTF starting share of 10%, 30% and 50%, beside the environment's own share (18% in Reference and Adverse, 8% in Stress; G against B). ## 6. Scale (addendum rule 5; the programme's rule 3)

Independent runs. A difference passes where |Δ| exceeds both the half-width of its interval and the materiality threshold (1 point; 5% of the 500-adult value for cost). Rows: the release row with the layer, its effect against none and the layer's effect, at 2,000 and 10,000 adults against 500 (J and G10 against G); and the synergy table with the layer, G10 against G (20 years), contrast by contrast. Listed: every Δ that passes, and the layer's effect on every claimable outcome.
